## Document 041: 20261109_投資週報_期中選舉藍色浪潮，AI基建疑慮升溫

Source PDF: all report pdf/20261109_投資週報_期中選舉藍色浪潮，AI基建疑慮升溫.pdf
Report source: Yuanta (投資週報)
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同
意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 11 月 09 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 期中選舉 「藍色浪潮」 ，AI 基建政策風險升溫 ：11 月 3 日美國期中選舉民主黨奪回參眾兩院，
德州新任州長揚言暫停資料中心建設， 市場擔憂電力、 用水與環境審查趨嚴，AI 資本支出前景
遭重新評價；加上 Fed 官員釋出續升息訊號，資金撤出高評價半導體與 AI 基建族群，美股科
技股大幅修正。短線政策雜音難消，但 AI 需求本質未變，後續觀察新國會實際立法方向。
◼ 科技財報喜憂參半， 台股賣壓沉重、 政府出手穩市：蘋果營收優於預期但毛利率受記憶體成本

壓抑，AMD 資料中心營收近倍增、高通財報後走弱。美股賣壓延燒，台股連日重挫、新台幣
貶破 33 元，行政院指示金管會機動因應並研議穩定股市措施，官股行庫與投信持續買超。看
法：短線波動仍大，但基本面與政策支撐有助指數逐步築底。
◼ 美國製造業續擴張，台灣通膨降溫：美國 10 月 ISM 製造業指數升至 55.5，連續第十個月擴
張，惟價格分項仍處高檔；台灣 10 月製造業 PMI 連續 13 個月擴張但速度趨緩，CPI 年增率
降至 2.33%。看法：美國景氣與通膨黏性支撐 Fed 偏鷹立場，台灣央行壓力相對減輕；後續
關注 10 月非農就業、11 月 10 日美國 CPI 及 12 月 FOMC 會議。
市場展望
◼ 科技股短線波動較大，中期看好現金流穩健的 AI 受惠企業：科技股中期成長趨勢仍具支撐，
惟短線宜重視個股分化，美國期中選舉帶來的政策不確定性，短期可能使科技股評價承壓，
惟大型雲端業者持續投入 AI 基礎建設，半導體及相關設備需求仍具中期成長動能，科技股整
體基本面仍具支撐，但短期應避免過度集中於高評價、高資本支出及高度依賴外部融資的企

業。優先關注具備技術競爭優勢、穩定營運現金流及較高投資回報能見度的企業。
◼ 中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債仍較具優勢：在美國政策風險升溫及科技股評價調整期
間，美國公債仍具投資組合避險功能，但考量通膨與能源價格的不確定性，現階段不建議延
長存續期間。中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債在兼顧票息收益、利率敏感度與資產配置
穩定性上仍較具優勢。信用債方面，營運現金流充足、槓桿適中及再融資需求可控的發行人
仍較為穩健，對 AI 相關企業則須進一步評估資本支出、合約現金流與債務到期結構。

市場焦點
美國
• 10月S&P Global綜合PMI初值由58.4降至57.6，雖較前月回落，但仍處高檔區間；服務業動
能略為放緩，製造業PMI則升至56.0，創近4年半新高。新訂單增速加快 ，顯示企業需求、投
資及庫存回補動能仍具韌性 ，然而企業售價與投入成本同步上升 ，反映需求改善 、供應鏈延
遲及成本轉嫁效應持續存在 ，通膨降溫進程仍具不確定性 。整體而言 ，市場關注焦點轉向通
膨與利率走勢，高利率環境恐維持較長時間，進而推高股債資產評價波動。
中國
• 中國國家統計局公布第三季 GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期 ，在出口與科技製造業帶動下 ，
短期成長可望維持穩定 ，但若內需未能有效改善 ，經濟成長動能恐難全面擴散 ，建議聚焦在
半導體、AI硬體、伺服器供應鏈 ，及高端製造相關產業 ，至於消費 、房地產及傳統內需類股 ，

仍需觀察後續刺激政策與消費信心回升情況。
法國
政局動盪
• 法國金融市場因各地抗議活動持續擴大 ，面臨股債雙跌的局面。市場憂心法國政府可能擴大支
出以平息抗議，加劇財政可持續性疑慮 。法國10年期公債殖利率一度突破 5%，法德利差亦擴
大至歐債危機以來高點。不過，歐洲央行系統性壓力綜合指標仍處於歷史相對低檔 ，遠低於歐
元區主權債務危機期間，短期內演變為新一輪主權債務危機的風險仍相對有限。

美國PMI維持強勁擴張，經濟成長續航但通膨壓力再升溫
• 企業需求強勁支撐美國景氣維持高檔擴張 ：美國10月綜合PMI初值由58.4降至57.6，雖略低於前月，但仍處於近五年來
的高位區間，顯示企業活動持續高速擴張；其中服務業 PMI為57.9、製造業PMI升至56.0並創53個月新高，新訂單增速
更創逾4年半最佳表現，反映內需穩健、出口需求改善與企業補庫存效應同步發酵，美國第四季經濟動能仍具韌性。
• 成本上升與供應瓶頸推升通膨風險： 企業售價漲幅創2022年8月以來最高，能源、運輸、金屬原料及薪資成本同步上揚 ，
加上供應鏈交期延長與訂單積壓擴大，使企業得以持續轉嫁成本，暗示未來數月消費者通膨可能再度偏離聯準會目標區
間，市場對利率維持較高水準的預期恐因此升溫。
• 後市觀察：景氣韌性有利獲利成長，但通膨可能推升殖利率、壓抑高評價資產。投資配置建議優先布局美國大型科技股 、

美國金融股及工業資本支出受惠族群，可透過NASDAQ 100 ETF、S&P 500 ETF及美國銀行股ETF參與企業獲利成長；
固定收益則以短天期投資級債為主，以降低利率波動風險。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.24

顧
海外資金流入美國市場，支撐美國資產吸引力
• 海外資金持續流入美國：美國財政部8月TIC資料顯示，
整體資金淨流入 1,998億美元，其中民間部門淨流入
1,785億美元，為主要資金來源；外資淨買進美國長
期證券3,007億美元，遠高於美國投資人加碼海外資
產216億美元，反映全球資金在經濟成長與流動性考
量下，對美國金融市場仍維持高度的配置需求。
• 全球資金偏好美國資產： 外資 8月淨買入美國股票
1,319億美元，高於美國公債與公司債 ，顯示海外投
資人仍看好美國企業獲利與經濟基本面 ，雖然短期國
庫券持有量下降，部分跨境美元資金亦出現波動，但
以10月近況來看長天期公債標售仍吸引境外央行 、
保險資金及大型資產管理機構參與，反映高殖利率水
準對長期資金仍具吸引力。在經濟成長維持韌性與企

業獲利支撐下，美國資產仍為全球資金配置的重要核
心市場。
• 市場看法：海外資金流入有助反映美國資產吸引力，
惟後續仍須觀察通膨 、Fed政策及企業獲利表現 。投
資布局建議可以美國投資等級債、美國中短天期公債
及大型AI基礎建設受惠族群為核心配置 ，兼顧收益、
防禦及長期成長潛力。

歷史數據顯示全球股市突破歷史新高後，後續上漲機率高
• 很多人直覺以為見高就是頂，但過去20年的統計資料顯示，全球股市在創下新高後，多數時間選擇繼續向上走。
• 勝率顯著偏高： 全球股市 (MSCI全球指數 )創新高後持有一年 ，獲得正報酬的機率高達 62%，三個月後的正報酬機率更達
71%。美股與台股表現最亮眼，標普500指數創高後一年上漲機率為76%，那斯達克為79%；而台灣股市表現更為驚人，創
高後三個月內上漲的機率高達82%。
• 下檔拉回風險有限：在創高後短期(一個月內)修正幅度其實相當溫和 ，全球多數主要市場的回徹幅度中位數僅 1%～2%左右。
• 突破前高優於連續創新高： 若市場在歷經至少一個月以上的整理後突破前高 ，其未來一年的平均報酬率高達 8.4%，遠高於

連續每天創新高的3.9%。這代表市場經適度整理後的突破，具備更健康的上漲動能。
創新高後的平均遠期報酬率
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.22

市場寬度收窄，由少數巨頭獨撐
• 當前的市場特徵由少數巨頭帶頭衝： 科技股權重較高的指數(如那斯達克、台股、韓股)，長期以來都有少數權重股拉動指數
的現象。自2024年以來，全球13個主要市場中有10個市場在創新高時，參與上漲的股票比例明顯下降 。已開發市場創新高
時高於200日均線的股票比例從過去 75.5%降至65.1%，新興市場更降至 61.3%。這意味著指數看似風光 ，但多數個股並未
同步創高，當漲勢過度集中於少數大型科技巨頭時，整體市場的抗風險能力會相對減弱。
• 小結與投資建議：歷史經驗顯示，創新高本身是強勢市場的特徵，後續繼續走揚的機率遠大於立刻崩盤。然而，鑑於目前市
場寬度收窄、權重高度集中於科技股的現狀 ，盲目追高個股的風險已顯著增加 。投資建議可以利用ETF掌握大盤趨勢，由於

指數上漲主要由龍頭股驅動 ，透過市值型ETF(如追蹤標普500ETF)能直接享受龍頭巨頭帶來的指數創高紅利 ，避免選錯個股
而賺了指數、賠了價差。此外，提防短期股市回檔，可運用定期定額分批布局或等待大盤適度拉回整理再行加碼，降低追高
風險。

法國政局動盪加劇財政壓力，股債同步承壓
• 法國政局動盪加劇財政壓力，股債同步承壓：法國各地抗議活動持續擴大，最初由學生發起，隨後因生活成本高漲而引發其
他群體響應。在財政空間有限、俄烏戰爭與美伊衝突推升能源價格的背景下，政府難以有效緩解民生成本壓力，法國金融市
場因此面臨股債雙跌的局面。
• 公債殖利率攀升，但系統性風險仍相對可控：市場憂心法國政府可能擴大支出以平息抗議，加劇財政可持續性疑慮。法國10
年期公債殖利率一度突破5%，法德利差亦擴大至歐債危機以來高點。不過，歐洲央行系統性壓力綜合指標（CISS）仍處於
歷史相對低檔，遠低於歐元區主權債務危機期間，顯示金融市場尚未出現廣泛的系統性壓力，短期內演變為新一輪主權債務
危機的風險仍相對有限。

日本央行本週料維持利率不變
日本央行利率走勢預估
資料來源： Bloomberg，2026.10.15 資料來源： tradingeconomics，2026.10.21
日本貿易收支
• 日本貿易逆差擴大，進口增幅高於出口：日本2026年9月貿易逆差擴大至5,366億日圓，較去年同期的2,777億日圓增加93%，
高於市場預期的1,810億日圓，連續第五個月呈現貿易赤字。出口年增16.7%至10.97兆日圓，受惠於半導體相關電子零件、
製造設備及汽車出口成長；惟進口年增18.9%至11.51兆日圓，增幅高於出口，主要受到原油、半導體電子零件及液化天然
氣進口增加帶動。其中，原油進口年增51.1%，部分反映日本在荷姆茲海峽局勢緊張下，積極分散能源採購來源。
• 日本央行本週料維持利率不變：日本央行9月將政策利率調升25個基點至1.25%，創31年來新高。考量前幾次升息對經濟的

傳導效果仍待觀察，加上貿易逆差擴大反映進口成本壓力，多數分析師預期日本央行本週將暫停升息，以評估貨幣緊縮對經
濟與通膨的影響。

中國國家統計局公布第三季GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期
• 中國國家統計局公布第三季GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期：顯示中國經濟在第二季落底後呈現溫和回升 ，從季度表現來看，
一、二、三季增速分別為5.0%、4.3%及4.5%，反映政策支持與外需復甦開始發揮效果 ，全年達成官方4.5%成長目標的機率
明顯提升，第三季經濟改善的主要動能來自出口 ，受惠於全球企業持續擴建 AI基礎設施，及科技產品需求強勁 ，中國高科技
產品出口表現亮眼 ，尤其是積體電路 、伺服器、電腦零組件，及儲存設備等產品增幅顯著 ，高技術製造業維持雙位數成長 。
同時，中國對美國及歐盟出口維持強勁 ，推升貿易順差 走升，為支撐經濟的重要力量 。然而，內需疲弱仍是當前經濟最大隱
憂，房地產調整尚未結束，居民消費與企業投資意願保守，使經濟復甦結構呈現「外熱內冷」的特徵。

• 展望後市：在出口與科技製造業帶動下 ，短期成長可望維持穩定 ，但若內需未能有效改善 ，經濟成長動能恐難全面擴散 ，建
議聚焦在半導體、AI硬體、伺服器供應鏈 ，及高端製造相關產業 ，至於消費、房地產及傳統內需類股 ，仍需觀察後續刺激政
策與消費信心回升情況。
資料來源：開源證券，2026.10.22

彭博預估騰訊、阿里巴巴在中國AI競爭中勝出機率較高
• 彭博預估騰訊、阿里巴巴在中國AI競爭中勝出機率較高：彭博產業研究認為，中國AI產業未來將由大型科技平台主導 ，其中騰
訊、字節跳動與阿里巴巴憑藉龐大用戶基礎 、數據資源、資金實力及完整生態系統 ，在AI競賽中具備明顯優勢 ，這些企業可將
AI廣泛應用於電商、社群、遊戲、金融與雲端業務，提升營運效率並強化競爭門檻，例如：阿里巴巴利用 AI 驅動電商平台上的
個人化產品推薦，而騰訊則運用 AI 優化短影音廣告並強化微信功能。騰訊自 2026 年 7 月起開始將混元 AI 模型整合至其電子
遊戲中，使遊戲角色及助理能更準確地回應玩家 ，然而，AI模型訓練與基礎建設投入龐大 ，獲利能力短期仍受壓抑 ，且須面對
監管、資料隱私及晶片供應等挑戰 。此外，智譜、月之暗面、MiniMax及商湯等AI新創雖具技術創新能力，但缺乏規模效益、

穩定現金流及長期資本支持，未來獲利挑戰較大。
• 投資建議：看好中國AI長期發展者，可優先關注具資金 、算力與應用場景優勢的大型平台企業 ，受惠程度可望高於多數 AI新創
公司。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.21

台灣9月外銷訂單再創新高，台股第四季有望先蹲後跳
• 台灣9月外銷訂單再創新高： 9月外銷訂單1,142.1億美元，連兩個月破千億美元 ，再創單月新高；年增率高達 62.4%，連續
20個月呈雙位數正成長 。經濟部表示，AI強勁需求超乎預期 ，預估今年第四季接單還會優於第三季 。展望10月表現，外銷
訂單將持續亮麗，估落在1,125億至1,159億美元，有望連三個月破千億美元。
• 台股第四季有望先蹲後跳： 10月初以來，台股在盤中觸及歷史高點 49,968點後面臨一波修正 ，主要反映在新一輪的財報公
布前，市場觀望AI投資是否存在泡沫化風險 。短期來看，台股在季線附近出現支撐 ，不過在11月初的美國期中選舉落幕前 ，
台股維持區間震盪的可能性較高；中期來看，美國期中選後往往因政治不確定性下降，歷史統計股市上漲機率高，加上台灣

年底集團作帳及選舉行情，正向看待年底台股表現。
資料來源：經濟部統計處，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.20。

1. 本資料內容僅供內部同仁及客戶參考，非作為任何金融商品或服務之購買或銷售之推介，不得視為買賣有價證券或其他金融商品之要約或要約之引誘。
2. 本資料提及之經濟走勢預測不必然代表基金之績效，基金投資風險請詳閱基金公開說明書。
3. 基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益 ，投資人申購
前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

市場焦點
美國
• 9月綜合PMI升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速 ，新訂單及就業亦
明顯成長 ，顯示消費 、AI投資與國防支出支撐景氣動能 。不過，需求快速擴張也加劇供應鏈
瓶頸，企業投入價格升至 2022年10月以來高點，燃料、運輸及原物料成本上升 ，顯示景氣韌
性與通膨壓力同步升溫，增加Fed政策維持緊縮的壓力。
美國
期中選舉
• 目前民調顯示 ，民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ，這將有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期 ，進
而帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑 ，對長天期美債形成支撐 。此外既有資料顯示 ，選後一年美股
平均報酬率達12.4%，主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到經濟成長、企業獲利及貨
幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。

越南
• 越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數 。估計此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流
入越南。資產管理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約 25億美元。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風 ，除了能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經濟成長 ，今年以來越南成為
美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成為中國商品流入的中轉國 ，美對越的301條款
調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。

美國景氣強勢擴張，通膨與利率壓力再升
• 企業活動創逾五年新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速擴張 ：S&P
Global美國9月綜合PMI初值升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高，
且連續四個月加速成長；服務業 PMI升至58.7（59個月高點）、
製造業PMI升至57.0（52個月高點），產出亦同步加速 ，加上
訂單與就業成長皆達近年最快 ，顯示內需、AI投資及國防支出
共同支撐企業活動。S&P Global依PMI調查推估經濟年成長約
5%，Q3則上看4%，反映短期景氣動能明顯增強。
• 供給受限推高成本 ，通膨壓力再升溫： 需求快速擴張同時推高
供應鏈與產能壓力 ，企業未完成訂單升至 2022年5月以來高點 ，
供應商交貨延遲亦明顯惡化 ，反映產能不足 ，投入價格指數升

至2022年10月以來新高 ，企業主要反映燃料 、運輸、原物料
及薪資成本上升 ，顯示供給受限與能源成本可能持續向終端價
格傳導，增加通膨黏著風險並提高Fed維持緊縮政策的壓力。
• 後市觀察：數據公布後，10年期美債殖利率一度升至約 5.12%，
創2007年以來新高 ，市場對10月再升息的押注亦升溫 ，短線
需關注供應鏈壓力是否緩解 ，以及後續ISM、就業與物價數據
能否確認通膨再升溫趨勢 。投資策略建議關注利率敏感度較低
的高品質成長股或防禦型資產 ，可適度布局受惠強勁內需與 AI
／國防支出的美股大型科技與工業類股 ，並留意升息環境下金
融股的利差受益機會 ，整體建議維持分散配置並嚴控利率風險 。
資料來源：S&P Global PMI, Bureau of Economic Analysis via S&P Global Market
Intelligence， 2026.9.24

能源成本升高推升通膨，利率維持高檔壓力升溫
• 能源與運輸成本上升，增加通膨黏著風險：中東衝突與
俄羅斯煉油設施遭攻擊 ，使全球柴油供應持續偏緊 ，燃
料價格及運輸成本同步上升；由於柴油廣泛應用於運輸 、
農業與製造業，價格上漲將透過物流及生產成本向下游
傳導。目前全球柴油庫存偏低 、煉油產能受限，若供應
中斷延續，可能使能源價格維持高檔並延長通膨壓力。
• 公債與企業債供給增加 ，長端利率壓力升高：美國財政
赤字及政府融資需求偏高 ，加上大型科技公司持續為 AI
資料中心等基礎建設籌資 ，債券供給增加使市場承接壓
力升高；在通膨預期未完全穩定的情況下 ，投資人對長
天期債券要求較高的期限溢酬 ，進一步推升長端殖利率 ，
同時AI相關企業債近期亦出現利差擴大 ，反映市場對大

量發債及資本支出回收期的關注升高。
• 資產配置面臨成長與通膨雙重拉扯： 能源價格、企業融
資成本及長端殖利率若持續處於高檔 ，將提高企業資金
成本並壓縮高估值資產的評價空間；同時 ，利率與能源
價格波動也可能擴大股 、債市震盪。因此資產配置建議
降低長天期債券對利率變化的敏感度 ，提高對通膨與利
率波動的防禦能力 ，並以現金流穩定、具備定價能力之
優質標的為核心。
資料來源：Federal Reserve Bank of St. Louis via FRED ， 2026.9.8

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.9

美國期中選舉-目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數
• 目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ：最新民調顯示，民主黨
於11月美國期中選舉，取得國會多數席次的機率已領先共和黨，根據市場
情境分析，期中選舉不影響既有法案執行 ，但將左右未來兩年的財政預算 、
稅制改革 、政府支出及國會監督權限 。情境(1)若共和黨續掌國會 ，減稅
政策延長、產業回流、關稅及移民限制等議程有望加速推進 ，財政擴張力
道可能進一步增強 。情境(2)分裂國會 ，兩黨制衡將使重大法案推動難度
提高，新增減稅與大型財政刺激方案較難落地 。情境(3)民主黨全面掌控
國會，則可能提高對政府政策的監督力度 ，政策方向偏向提高稅負與監管 ，
壓抑企業獲利 ，使政策環境趨向平衡。

美國期中選舉-對於股債市的影響
• 對股市的影響：美國銀行分析1900至2025年共31次美國期中選舉，資料發現標普500在期中選舉前12個月平均報酬約為2.9%，
低於所有年度平均 8.9%。因為市場在選舉前往往受到政策不確定性 、政黨競爭及投資人觀望情緒影響 ，波動相對較大 。然而
此現象不代表選舉必然導致市場疲弱，因為當時的經濟環境、通膨、利率及企業獲利等因素同樣扮演重要角色。期中選舉後12
個月的股市表現通常較佳 。歷史數據顯示，選後一年美股平均報酬率達12.4%。主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到
經濟成長、企業獲利及貨幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。
• 對債市的影響：若民主黨取得國會主導權，市場普遍預期財政支出擴張幅度將較受節制 ，有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期，進而

帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑，對長天期美債形成支撐，若共和黨持續全面掌控國會，市場可能預期減稅及財政擴張政策延續，
進一步推升財政赤字預估，帶動公債殖利率走高，對長天期美債形成壓力，不過市場已部分反映相關預期，實際衝擊幅度有限。
美國期中選舉前後對於美股的影響
資料來源：美國銀行，2026.7
資料來源：復華投信，2026.9

AI建設熱潮吞噬自由現金流
• 資本支出創新高：雲端服務巨頭(包括亞馬遜、Alphabet、微軟、Meta、甲骨文以及SpaceX等)正在全力擴充AI算力與資料
中心，2026年大型雲端服務巨頭的資本支出合計預估將接近 9,600億美元，而2025至2031年的累計AI資本支出更可能逼近
9兆美元。
• 自由現金流出現赤字： 高額的投資加上股東回報(股息與庫藏股買回)，使許多巨頭的自由現金流(FCF)轉為負值(如Alphabet、
亞馬遜、微軟、Meta及甲骨文等)，預計到2027年，部分企業將更加依賴外部融資，而非單靠內部現金生成。
• 債券發行量大增： 為了填補資金缺口 ，科技業債券發行規模急遽上升 ，2026年迄今，各幣別的投資級科技債券發行量已突
破4,000億美元，創下歷史新高。

獲利韌性與金庫深度：強勁EBITDA成為信用安全網
• 上兆美元的流動性護城河： 儘管債務增加，但科技十二巨頭合計持有近 1兆美元的等值現金，整體科技板塊流動性充裕 ，短
期內無流動性危機。
• EBITDA強勁成長消化債務：市場預期覆蓋的12家最大科技企業在2026與2027年的EBITDA合計將成長約35%，強勁的獲利
能力顯著降低槓桿比率的上升壓力，確保信評機構維護穩定的信用評級。
• 資本配置策略分化：(1)兼顧股東回報：蘋果、微軟、Meta與Alphabet在投入AI同時，仍維持大規模的庫藏股買回與股息發
放。(2)全數投入資本支出：亞馬遜將絕大部分現金導向資本支出(2026年資本支出預計達2,200億美元居首)，且不發放股息。
• 小結與投資建議：科技巨頭為維持AI技術與基礎設施領先優勢，短期內，負自由現金流將成為常態。然而，這並非財務危機

訊號，而是企業利用超強獲利能力 (高EBITDA)與龐大的現金儲備進行戰略性擴張 ，科技板塊的信用風險目前處於可控且健
康的狀態，投資建議聚焦AI變現能力與EBITDA成長，優先佈局具有強大獲利護城河且能將算力轉化為實質營收的龍頭企業 。

越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數
• 預估引導60億美元資金流入越南： 富時羅素估計，此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流入越南 。受此提振，資產管
理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約25億美元。整個指數過渡將分為四個階段推行至2027年：
今年9月率先納入10%，明年3月追加20%，最後於明年6月與9月分別完成剩餘各35%的分配。市場分析指出，隨著明年3月
配額加大，市場關注度與對本地投資人的影響將更為顯著。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風： 儘管官方早前誓言今年 GDP成長目標達10%，但中東衝突以來能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經
濟成長。此外，越南與美國的貿易協商仍在進行中，由於今年以來越南成為美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成

為中國商品流入的中轉國，美對越的301條款調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。
• 觀點：儘管短線面臨不確定性 ，但越南股市同時具備多項利多題材 ，包含企業獲利成長、公共投資加速、FDI回流及資本市
場改革等。中長期結構仍偏正向，可以衛星配置的角度布局，或是以定期定額的方式參與長期成長潛力。

chrischenlc@yuanta.com

客戶缺料及平台轉換影響拉貨，4Q26 EPS 預估季減 22% 
川湖 3Q26 營收 173.38 億元，季增 60.1%、年增 296.9%，低於預期 7.3%。
7–8 月稼動率 80%，9 月降至 60%，主因客戶記憶體缺料、新舊平台轉換及
新創客戶一次性建置完成，因此維持 3Q26 毛利率預估 87.2%，EPS 下修
7.2%至 119.52 元。4Q26 營收則以 9 月作為基準，客戶拉貨取決於急缺零
組件到位情況，備料完成隨時啟動拉貨。預估 4Q26 營收 135.61 億元，季
減 21.8%，毛利率 86.4%，EPS 下修 28.2%至 92.91 元，季減 22.3%，反
映客戶備料及平台轉換影響。 
1H27 VR 量產與周邊機櫃導入，台美擴產支應成長 
1H27 進入 VR 量產週期，除運算機櫃外，電源、CPU 等周邊 Side Rack 同
步導入，擴大導軌應用並提高單一系統供貨價值。新平台設計與加工難度提
升，帶動高階導軌量價成長。擴產方面，美國休士頓廠已出貨，川湖二廠仍
在興建，預計 2H27 投產；三、五期目前處於設計階段，預計 2027 年動工。
三期規模相當於現有廠房面積，五期與三期相同，擴建完成後整體產能至少
為現有 3 倍。因規模較大，建廠需 2–2.5 年，預計 2029 年底至 2030 年完
工。本中心預估 2027 年營收 730.53 億元，年增 54.8%，EPS 500.57 元。 
維持川湖買進評等，目標價 15,000 元 
考量記憶體缺料、新舊平台轉換及新創客戶一次性建置完成， 本次下修
2026／2027 年營收預估 12.3%／13.7%至 471.78／730.53 億元，年增
169.6%／54.8%；EPS 下修 12.4%／14.0%至 323.39／500.57 元，年增
213.3%／54.8%。看好川湖導軌製造能力， 1H27 VR 量產及周邊機櫃導入
帶動需求，二廠 2H27 投產增加產能，三、五期支應中長期成長。維持
2027 年 30 倍本益比，以 EPS 500.57 元推得目標價 15,000 元，維持買進。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
記憶體缺料、平台轉換及新創客戶一次性建置完成，影響短期拉貨，
下修 3Q26／4Q26 EPS 7%／28%至 119.52／92.91 元。 
⧫  
1H27 VR 量產與周邊機櫃導入帶動高階導軌需求，川湖二廠 2H27 投
產增加供應能力，預估 2027 營收 730.53 億元，年增 54.8%。 
⧫  
下修 2026／2027 年 EPS 預估 12%／14%至 323.39 元／500.57 元，
維持 2027 年 30 倍本益比，目標價調整至 15,000 元，維持買進。

約 80%，9 月降至 60%，主要受到 1）客戶記憶體缺料，影響整機備料及組裝進度；2）新舊平台
轉換；3）部分 AI 新創客戶一次性建置完成等因素影響，導致 9 月營收月減約 30%。本中心維持

客戶備料進度影響拉貨節奏，4Q26 營收預估季減 22%
公司表示，客戶拉貨節奏相當不規律，主要取決於急缺零組件取得情況；當記憶體等材料到位、整
機具備組裝條件後，即可啟動拉貨，因此單月出貨仍將隨客戶備料進度波動。目前客戶專案需求預
測未見明顯異常，但缺料與新舊平台轉換使需求釋出時點較難平均分布。
本中心下修 4Q26 營收預估 27.9%至 135.61 億元，季減 21.8%、年增 174.0%；考量出貨規模及
產能利用率調整，毛利率預估下修 0.8 個百分點至 86.4%，季減 0.8 個百分點。營業利益預估
109.79 億元，季減 22.6%、年增 220.6%；稅後淨利預估 88.54 億元，季減 22.3%、年增 152.0%，

EPS 預估下修 22.3%至 92.91 元。

1H27 進入 VR 量產週期，周邊 Side Rack 擴大導軌需求
1H27 進入 Vera Rubin 量產週期，除運算機櫃外，電源、CPU 等周邊 Side Rack 亦須配合整體系
統導入，導軌需求由運算設備延伸至周邊基礎設施。成長來源除新平台出貨增加，亦包含不同功能
機櫃增加，以及導軌設計與加工難度提高，推升單一系統的供貨價值。

各類導軌依機構設計與加工要求定價，部分電源及 CPU 應用的導軌複雜度高於 GPU 應用，產品價
值不隨設備售價等比例下降。川湖既有產品原則上於生命週期內維持議定價格，新世代產品則依升
級規格重新報價，因此平均單價提升主要來自新產品導入及產品組合優化。本中心看好 VR 量產、
ASIC 平台升級及周邊應用增加，帶動 2027 年高階導軌量價成長，預估全年營收 730.53 億元，年
增 54.8%，毛利率 86.5%，年增 0.6 個百分點，EPS 500.57 元。
美國休士頓廠已於 10 月投產；川湖二廠則預計 2H27 投產
川湖二廠目前仍在興建，預計 2H27 投產；美國休士頓廠則已於 9–10 月向首批客戶出貨，完整產
能開出後將增加整體產能約 20%，目前生產效率約為台灣廠的一半，公司透過人員訓練及製程改
善提高效率，2027 年持續提升產出。部分 AI 專案交期急迫，空運成本甚至高於導軌本身，北美在
地生產可縮短交付時間，支援客戶急單及補貨需求。休士頓廠產出提升與二廠 2H27 投產，將分階

段增加供應能力，配合 VR 及其他新平台的出貨需求。
川益三、五期預計 2027 年動工，2029 年底至 2030 年完工
川益三、五期已開始設計，後續須完成審圖及發包，預計 2027 年動工。三期規模相當於目前所有
廠房面積的加總，五期則與三期規模相同；依公司說明，擴建完成後整體產能至少達現有的 3 倍。
由於工程規模較大，建廠時間約需 2–2.5 年，預計 2029 年底至 2030 年完工，支應後續新世代平
台及周邊應用需求。
公司已通過三、五期廠房新建及設備採購預算 197.12 億元，依工程與設備採購進度分期投入，並
規劃發行面額上限合計 100 億元、年期 5 年、票面利率 0%的可轉債，資金用於轉投資川益科技。
三、五期定位為中長期產能布局，2027 年新增供應主要來自休士頓廠產出提升及二廠投產。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：組裝及零組件產業 2026 年 11 月 4 日 King Slide 第4 頁，共 20頁  
產品結構分析  
預估川湖 2027 年 AI 伺服器營收比重達 84%，多元平台架構支撐長期展望 
川湖具備高承載、高耐用度及客製化滑軌設計能力，AI 伺服器對於滑軌承重、抗震與散熱協同設計
要求提升，形成新進者難以突破的技術門檻。川湖目前 2Q26 產品組合為伺服器 94%、6%，本中
心預估川湖 2026/2027 年 AI 伺服器營收比重分別達 78%/84%，其中以 GB/VR/ASIC 系列為主軸，
並擴產至 NVDIA Groq、Spectrum-X 交換器、AMD MI450 以及 Neo Cloud 等新平台架構。 
展望 2026-2027 年，我們預估川湖於整體伺服器導軌供貨份額達 90%以上，並看好川湖伺服器導
軌成長動能無虞，主因包含 1) GB300 單一機櫃導軌產值估較 GB200 成長 80%，數量由 2.5 萬櫃
提升至 6.8 萬櫃；2) Vera Rubin(VR200) 平台預計 2H26 登場，預計 4Q26 量產，預估 2027 年達
7-8 萬櫃；3) AWS T3 需求強勁，預期 AWS ASIC 晶片量將 2026/2027 年分別年增 25%/70%至
200/340 萬顆；4) 受惠於 AI 訓練與推論需求爆發，預估 2026 年通用型伺服器出貨量將年增 23%。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：組裝及零組件產業 2026 年 11 月 4 日 King Slide 第5 頁，共 20頁  
同業比較分析  
川湖為 AI 導軌龍頭廠商，專利護城河將確保公司穩居領先地位 
川湖在歐美、台灣及中國皆有完整的專利佈局，目前專利核准數高達 3,641 件，深厚的專利壁壘使
競爭對手極難繞過，無法輕易生產高可靠度產品。同時，川湖具備高承載、高耐用度及客製化滑軌
設計能力，能完美對應 AI 伺服器對滑軌承重、抗震與散熱協同設計的嚴格要求，形成新進者難以
突破的技術門檻。在市場定位與市佔表現方面，對比國內同業南俊國際（ 6584 TT ）在 GB 及 
ASIC 等 AI 導軌出貨比重仍低，川湖的整體伺服器導軌市佔率預估高達 90% 以上，專利護城河將
確保公司穩居領先地位；就獲利率而言，由於川湖的  AI 導軌營收佔比較高，整體獲利能力亦優於
南俊國際。 
我們持續看好公司憑藉極高的技術門檻與產品可靠度，產品線全面涵蓋次世代  AI 伺服器（如  
GB300、Vera Rubin 平台）、ASIC 伺服器、網路交換器及 Neo Cloud 等多元平台，以因應強勁
需求。此外，川湖的擴廠計畫包含：1）美國德州新廠已於 2026 年 10 月開始出貨；2）台灣二廠
預估於 2H27；3）川益三期、五期預期 2029 年底至 2030 年完工，預期將支撐中長期成長。

財務分析  
川湖整體財務槓桿維持極低且健全的水準，負債比長年保持在  20% 以下，顯著低於同業平均，展
現強健的抗風險能力；流動比率則維持在 5.5 至 8 倍，優於同業平均之表現，顯示公司流動性充裕、
短期償債無虞。在存貨管理方面，存貨周轉天數近年約落在  100 至 150 天區間震盪，略高於同業
平均。受惠於產品組合優化與高毛利訂單放量，ROE 與 ROA 呈現逐年走揚態勢，營運效率持續改
善。整體而言，本中心認為川湖在極佳的資本結構與流動性保護下，獲利能力與資產運用效率同步
提升，財務體質維持高度穩健。

### Page 8

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：組裝及零組件產業 2026 年 11 月 4 日 King Slide 第8 頁，共 20頁  
產業概況  
AI、ASIC、通用型強勢出擊，2026 年美系 CSP 資本支出估年增 96%  
在 ChatGPT 等 AI 應用陸續推向市場，各大業者紛紛開始研發自有超級大模型，包括 Gemini 
(Google)、Claude (Anthropic)、Llama (Meta) 等，各大品牌開始大舉投入大型語言模型(LLM)市場，
且每一個產品的智慧皆持續提高，部分消費者也陸續從 ChatGPT 陣營轉移至 Gemini 陣營，反映
出消費者開始逐漸提升對 AI 的黏著度。根據 Grand View Research 調查，2024 年全球 AI 市場營
收約 2,792 億美元，預估 2030 年市場規模超過 3 兆美元，擁有超過 30% 的年複合增長率。 
面對這爆發性的長期成長趨勢，CSP 業者(Amazon、Microsoft、Google、Meta)在法說會上一致強
調，AI 投資已轉變為一項橫跨 2026 年的結構性戰略，CSP 大廠不僅持續擴大對 GB300 等次世代
AI 伺服器的採購，更將重點放在資料中心基礎設施的長期戰略規劃，以應對 AI 運算對電力及散熱
系統強勁成長的需求，確保未來數年內擁有足夠的能源與容量來支持持續迭代的大模型部署。本中
心認為，2026 年美國主要 CSP 資本支出將年增 96%，且將有一半的成長來自於資料中心建置，代
表了資料中心的不足及未來伺服器需求。明年不僅僅是 NVIDIA GB300 的天下，AWS、Google、
Meta ASIC 的崛起也將是一大關鍵，其中又以 Google TPU 需求最為強勁，已經有兩個客戶預計將
採購 TPU。 
另外，受惠於 AI 訓練與推論需求爆發的連帶效應，本中心預估 2026 年通用型伺服器出貨量將年增
23%，其中以存儲型伺服器需求最為強勁。隨著生成式 AI 應用普及和使用者數量暴增，CSP 等業
者必須不斷擴充容量，且不僅限於高階 GPU 伺服器，龐大的 AI 模型訓練和推論在使用狀態中將消
耗大量的數據(DATA)，使得存儲型伺服器的需求大增。

### Page 9

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：組裝及零組件產業 2026 年 11 月 4 日 King Slide 第9 頁，共 20頁  
ASIC 進入高速成長期，GPU 需求仍維持擴張 
從 AI 晶片預期出貨來看，GPU 與 ASIC 需求均持續成長，但 2026 年後 ASIC 成長動能明顯加速。
Nvidia GPU 出貨量預估由 2025 年 565.7 萬顆增至 2026 年 650 萬顆、2027 年 850 萬顆，年增
15%及 31%；AMD 亦由 2025 年 90 萬顆增至 2027 年 180 萬顆，顯示通用 GPU 算力需求仍維持
成長。 
相較之下，ASIC 受惠 CSP 加速自研晶片及導入規模擴大，總出貨量預估由 2025 年 380 萬顆大幅
增至 2026 年 725 萬顆、2027 年 1,360 萬顆，年增率達 91%及 88%，成長速度明顯高於 GPU。其
中 Google 仍為最大 ASIC 使用者， 2027 年用量預估達 800 萬顆，AWS 亦增至 320 萬顆，
Microsoft 及 Meta 同步擴大部署。我們認為，在資料中心電力逐漸成為算力擴張瓶頸下， CSP 將
更重視每瓦運算效能及整體運算成本， ASIC 針對特定運算工作的效率優勢有望推升滲透率，惟
GPU 憑藉通用性及軟體生態系優勢，仍將維持重要地位。

獲利調整與股票評價
維持川湖買進評等，目標價 15,000 元
考量客戶記憶體缺料、新舊平台轉換及新創客戶一次性建置完成，本次下修 2026／2027 年營收預
估 12.3%／13.7%至 471.78／730.53 億元，年增 169.6%／54.8%；毛利率分別下修 0.4／0.4 個

入帶動高階導軌需求，二廠 2H27 投產增加供應能力，三、五期則支應中長期成長。維持 2027 年
30 倍本益比，以 EPS 500.57 元推得目標價 15,000 元，維持買進評等。

公司簡介  
川湖為 AI 發展浪潮下之主要伺服器導軌受惠廠商  
川湖科技成立於 1986 年，2000-2001 年進入伺服器市場，目前伺服器、網路通訊、雲端資料中心及其週邊設備產品的比重
最高，其次是廚具相關產品，而鉸鏈、滑軌產品主要用於傢俱、鋼櫃、工具櫃、木製傢俱等產品。川湖員工數約 1,500 人，
廠房位於高雄市路竹區(川湖總部)及高雄科學園區(川益科技)。2026 年第二季營收比重中，伺服器業務佔營收約 94%、廚
具與其他業務則約為 6%。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 川湖整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於中等風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於略為領先
的位置，在電子零組件行業的公司中排名略微領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 川湖的整體曝險屬於中等水準，略為落後電子零組件行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 
議題包含營運方面的碳排、公司在生產中使用原材料的效率(不包括能源和石油產品)、人力資源等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：川湖在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬上等。公司未曾涉入重大財務事件。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

## Document 109: 261029_daiwa_ASE

Source PDF: all report pdf/261029_daiwa_ASE.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: ASE disclosed a strong set of quarterly results and guidance 
on 29 October, both beating market expectations, thanks to the strong AI 
scaling which is just getting stronger to benefit ASE’s leading-edge 
advanced packaging (LEAP) business. We raise our forecasts and 12M TP 
and reaffirm our Outperform (2) call on the stock; accumulate into any 
technical pullback.

What’s the impact: Results and guidance all beat. ASE reported 3Q26 
net profit of c.TWD27bn, beating our/consensus estimates by 25%/18% 
thanks to higher semiconductor assembly & test (SAT, or IC ATM by 
company definition) revenue which came in at TWD151bn (USD4.7bn), 7% 
above our estimate, for higher gross margin (GM) of 30.5%, 1.9pp above 
ours and topping ASE’s structural target of 24-30%. The strength was 
amplified further by lower non-operating losses, resulting in the strong 
bottom-line beat (table on p.2). Guidance was another beat thanks to the 
strong SAT business where ASE expects revenue to grow 11-13% QoQ for 
GM to top its record of 30.5% during the COVID upturn. We raise our 2026-
28E EPS by 14-24% assuming its SAT GM to reach 33% through 2027.

Strong AI gets stronger. Discussions centred on ASE’s LEAP business, 
where management further hiked revenue target this year beyond USD4bn 
(USD1.6bn in 2025), after 3 hikes YTD, thanks to strong demand for its on-
substrate (oS) packaging and initial ramp of its full-process solutions. With 
addition of new co-packaged optics (CPO) solutions, ASE expects the 
LEAP revenue to double YoY in 2027. As LEAP is margin-accretive, ASE 
expects the SAT GM to continue improving beyond 4Q26, making the 
ceiling of its structural GM target now as the floor to kick off 2027 for a 
strong start. Given management’s emphasis on “friendly pricing”, we expect 
likely continued cost passthrough across the supply chain to add to GM 
upside. Working hard to narrow the supply/demand gap, ASE further raised 
this year’s capex to USD11.5bn (from USD10.5bn), reinforcing our view 
that AI is scaling strongly to accelerate the chip industry growth.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we raise our 12M TP 
for ASE to TWD710 (previous: TWD550), based on our revised ROE-
adjusted PBR target of 5.8x (previous: 5.2x) 2027E BVPS due to a higher 
ROE trajectory. Our revised TP translates into 24x 4-quarter forward PER, 
which we see as justifying the rich PBR target given its 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 25%. We reiterate our Outperform (2) call with key downside risks 
of any “silicon inflation” and geopolitical overhang leading to AI capex cut.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 5-10% above the consensus as we 
believe the street has yet to factor in its stronger LEAP business.

29 October 2026

Entering an even stronger earnings cycle (III)

➢ 3Q26 results and 4Q26 guidance all beat expectations 
➢ Strong AI scaling is getting even stronger to benefit LEAP 
➢ Reaffirm Outperform (2) call with a higher 12M TP of TWD710

Company profile 
Established in April 2018, ASE Industrial Holding Co (HoldCo) is a holding company which privatized 
ASE and SPIL fully on 30 April 2018 through equity and cash swap, respectively, and listed on the same 
day with senior management assigned mainly by ASE. HoldCo has become the strong No.1 OSAT 
maker in the world, offering a comprehensive product portfolio to its customers from wirebonding, flip-
chip & bumping, system-in-package (SiP) to broad-based electronics manufacturing services (EMS).

### Page 5

5

ASE Technology Holding (3711 TT): 29 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 5 Feb 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
ASE’s Board of Directors consists of nine members with diverse professional backgrounds.
Three of the directors are independent, representing 33 percent of the board. The Chairman also
serves as the Chief Executive Officer, resulting in unified leadership and centralized decision-
making. To maintain effective governance and oversight under this structure, ASE relies on its
independent directors and established board processes. The board currently includes one
female director, and the company continues to pursue long-term goals related to board diversity
and balanced representation. ASE has an Audit Committee, Compensation Committee, Risk
Management Committee, and Chief Government Officer to conduct effective supervision.
Capital management 3
In the past 5 years, ASE paid over 50% or nearly 50% of its earnings in dividends. However, the
dividend trend was volatile in dollar and payout ratio terms. We forecast its dividend payout ratio
to average over 50% in the next 2 years.
Related party &
transaction 2
ASE undertakes related-party transactions with its subsidiaries on sales and purchases. The
total related-party transaction sales/purchase amounts were less than 10% of its total
sales/purchases, and the transaction terms with related parties were normal compared with
those with other customers/suppliers. Thus, we do not think related-party transactions have a
meaningful impact on overall operations.
S Supply chain management 2
The supply of raw materials has the most direct impact on ASE's operations and manufacturing.
To lower overall supply-chain risks, ASE has expanded the scope of sustainability risk
management to non-tier-1 suppliers. ASE currently has over 600 non-tier-1 suppliers.
ASE requires its suppliers to comply with its Supplier Code of Conduct, which aims to ensure
that suppliers conduct business in a manner that meets labour, health & safety, environment,
business ethics & management, and various corporate compliance standards.
E Water & wastewater management 2
ASE has adopted 3 water-use strategies of reduce, reuse and recycle. Through its water
management measures, it reduced water consumption density (water consumption/revenue) by
43% in 2024 vs. that of 2015. ASE is committed to increasing water reuse at its facilities in order
to improve water efficiency.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
ASE is committed to improving waste recycling and reuse through the 5 key elements of a
circular economy of: direct recycling, reuse, off-site regeneration, renewable materials, and
supply and rental. The recycling rate of general waste was 90% in 2024 and its hazardous waste
density declined by 53% in 2024 vs. that of 2015.
E GHG emissions 1
ASE is committed to achieving net zero emissions by advancing low carbon energy adoption,
developing smart grid technologies, and strengthening high tech energy management systems.
As of 2024, 19% of total manufacturing energy consumption comes from renewable sources,
88% of ASE facilities use renewable energy, and 10 sites have achieved RE100 certification.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

3Q26 earnings beat on better margins: See Results Summary
4Q26 revenue seen rising QoQ: Management guided for  4Q26 revenue to grow
QoQ, driven by power related products in Cloud & AIoT. IT/CE should continue to
decline YoY, despite strong growth from the LEO (low earth orbit). Optoelectronics
should grow by 5-10% YoY. Overall, we estimate revenue will grow by 5% QoQ in
4Q26 and GM will expand further to 26.1% (+0.2ppt QoQ) on a more favorable

revenue mix.
2027 expected to be another solid year: Looking to 2027, management holds an
optimistic view, guiding for the Cloud & AIoT business to grow by ~70% YoY,
Optoelectronics to grow by ~10% YoY and IT/CE business to stay roughly flat YoY.
AI remains the major driver, and should benefit from the growing AI deployment and
the spec upgrade of power-related products.
800VDC power rack under qualification: Lite-On sent its 800VDC power rack for

qualification in August and will have the next round of integrated system
qualification in November, aiming to be MP ready in 1Q27. That said, shipments will
depend upon customer deployment plans. Management expects only small volume
shipments in 1H27, and said visibility into the adoption in the new generation ASIC/
GPU platform from 2H27 remains to be seen, consistent with the consensus that
HVDC is more of a 2028 story at the earliest.
More offerings in the pipeline: Management sent DC/DC module samples to CSP

for qualification and expects to see some revenue contribution from 3Q27 (not
meaningful, though). Its 120KW CDU has obtained qualification in 3Q26 and should
see more volume from 4Q26, with the next generation 220KW CDU currently in the
development stage. In September 2026, it invested in a 25% stake in DCX, a liquid
cooling solution supplier in Poland, to strengthen its capability in the cooling space .
Stay EW: We think near-term sentiment should be relatively positive given the solid

3Q26 results, especially after the recent share price correction. Looking ahead, we
think Lite-On will grow along with the AI capex cycle, driven by its power-related
offerings, though execution and progress with new customers is worth monitoring.

Results Summary
3Q26 beat on better margins
Off revenue of NT$57,403mn (+9% QoQ/+28% YoY), GM contracted by 1.3ppt QoQ to
25.9% (vs. MSe/consensus at 24.6%/25.4%). The sequential decline was due to a high base
in 2Q26, which was elevated due to some one-offs. Opex was well controlled at NT
$6,211mn (opex ratio 10.8%), lower than our estimate of NT$6,851mn (opex ratio 11.5%)
and consensus of NT$7,097mn (opex ratio 11.8%)
Operating profit came in at NT$8,655mn (+6% QoQ/+85% YoY; OPM 15.1%),11% and 5%

higher than MSe of NT$7,807mn (OPM 13.1%) and consensus of NT$8,210mn (OPM
13.6%), respectively.
With non-op gains of NT$1,142mn (including FX gain of NT$734mn), net profit was NT
$7,429mn (+4% QoQ/+60% YoY; EPS NT$3.26), 18% and 11% above MSe (NT$6,313mn;
EPS NT$2.77) and consensus (NT$6,717mn; EPS NT$2.92).

Valuation Methodology
Our price target (base case scenario value) is NT$285 (prior NT$315), derived from 15x
2027-28e P/E (previously 17x): We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology
because we believe it appropriately captures Lite-On's earnings growth potential. We use
a lower target multiple to reflect more conservative sentiment toward the AI space in the
near term. We believe our target P/E multiple of 15x is justified by an earnings CAGR of

50% seen over 2025-28, which implies a PEG of 0.3. This is lower than the average of 1.6
in 2020-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 14x for a 9% earnings CAGR.
Bull case scenario value – NT$490 (previously NT$500): We apply a 25x P/E multiple
(previously 27x) to our average base case estimate for  2027-28. This scenario assumes
better-than-expected margin expansion, driven by operating efficiency and accelerating
production automation, while new business revenue comes in earlier than expected.

Strength from cloud computing and auto electronics is greater than expected.
Bear case scenario value – NT$190 (previously NT$200):  We apply a 10x P/E multiple
(previously 11x) to our average base case estimate for  2027-28. This scenario assumes
weaker-than-expected operating efficiency improvement, while pricing competition
increases amid macro volatility; auto electronics takes longer to pick up.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
(-) PC OEMs and ODMs: Intel (covered by Joseph Moore) expects 2H26 PC units to
fall 20%+ y/y as OEMs aggressively cut inventories amid weaker sell-through; the
correction has accelerated over the past two months. This aligns with Morgan

Stanley's estimate at a ~25% y/y decline  (report link).
(+) Server OEMs and ODMs: Intel guides for server CPU unit growth of 30%+ in
both 2026 and 2027, with upside if wafer/substrate supply improves. It sees server
CPU TAM rising from US$40bn today to >US$200bn in five years, driven mainly by
AI agents.
(+) ABF substrates:  Most of the previously discussed ~40% capacity increase is due
in 2H27; Intel expects key component purchase commitments to rise sharply over

the next several quarters. Intel is also prioritizing securing substrate capacity and
improving yields for the EMIB-T. Complex designs add yield risk, which could lower
effective output, worsening ABF substrate shortages but adding further support for
pricing.

Key Takeaways
We view negative PC comments as more bearish for OEMs and ODMs positioned
in the lower end consumer segment, like Acer and Compal.
Strong CPU unit growth would be positive for Wiwynn, Lenovo, and Lotes.
Tighter ABF substrate supply and stronger pricing would be positive for
Unimicron and NYPCB's revenue and margins.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 154: 261103_daiwa_sunon

Source PDF: all report pdf/261103_daiwa_sunon.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: We provide a 3Q26 results preview on Sunon, which plans to 
release its 3Q26 results on 6 November. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call as we 
expect the 4Q26 and 2027 outlook for its telecom, servers, industrial and 
auto segments to remain intact with both gross and operating margin set to 
expand. We now forecast a net profit CAGR of 28.2% over 2026-28E from 
21.2% previously (see our flash, 28 August).

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results preview. Sunon’s 3Q26 revenue of 
TWD6,143m (+13.4% QoQ and +24.5% YoY), a record-high level, 
accounted for 97.2% and 102.2%, respectively, of our previous 
(TWD6,319m, +16.6% QoQ and +28.1% YoY) and the Bloomberg 
consensus estimates for 3Q26 revenue. We forecast its 3Q26 gross margin 
to be 32.8% (vs. 31.3% in 2Q26 and 31.3% in 3Q25), slightly higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus estimate of 31.9%. We forecast an operating 
profit of TWD1,115m (+33.3% QoQ and +45.0% YoY), 3.0% higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus estimate of TWD1,082m and an operating 
margin of 18.1% (vs. 15.4% in 2Q26 and 15.6% in 3Q25). Overall, we 
forecast a net profit of TWD901m (+30.5% QoQ, +29.7% YoY), 1.9% 
higher than the consensus estimate of TWD884m. We forecast 4Q26 
revenue to grow 7.5% QoQ, and 1Q27 revenue to grow 1.4% QoQ 
considering its ASIC and GPU projects.

Rising liquid cooling revenue and EC fan exposure in 2027E underpin 
its gross margin expansion. We expect Sunon’s cold plate products (ie, 
enterprise and GPU clients), pump and CDU to be the key revenue drivers 
with certification also underway for CSP clients. We expect revenue from 
EC fans to reach 10% in 2027E for the industrial segment. We revise up 
our 2026-28E EPS by 1-13%, mainly on higher revenue, gross margin and 
operating margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD265 (from TWD236), still based on a target PER of 18x vs. its past-
5-year PER range of 6-25x, on the 2027E EPS, given solid earnings 
visibility from intact demand in the server, telecom, industrial and auto 
segments. We believe its dividend yield of 5.6-9.2% over 2026-28E will 
offer downside support for its share price. Downside risks: worse-than-
expected gross-margin expansion and AI server development progress.

How we differ: We are one of only two overseas brokers covering the 
stock. Our 2026-28E EPS are 4-12% above the Bloomberg consensus, 
likely due to our more aggressive revenue, gross margin and operating 
margin estimates.

3 November 2026

3Q26 results preview: ongoing gross margin expansion

➢ Rising liquid cooling and EC fans revenue mix in 2027E vs. 2026 
➢ Likely upward revision in EPS forecasts in the near term 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising 12M TP to TWD265

Company profile 
Founded in 1980 and listed on Taiwan’s main board in 2000, Sunonwealth Electric Machine Industry 
(Sunon) is one of the top-4 fan and motor providers in the world, along with Delta Electronics, Nidec and 
ebm-papst. Its major clients are Apple, Dell, Lenovo, Bosch, Siemens and Tesla. Its applications include 
IT & OE, telecoms & servers, industrial & medical equipment, home applications, distribution, autos and 
LED lighting.

### Page 5

5

Sunonwealth Electric Machine Industry (2421 TT): 3 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 8 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and chairperson of Sunonwealth are the same individual; Sunonwealth's board is led
by the Hung family. We see no issues with the company’s board. We view the chairman leading
the R&D team as a sign of the company’s continuing focus on developing new products to
achieve a long-term competitive advantage.
Capital management 2 Sunonwealth's dividend payout ratio was 83-88% in 2015-18, but it decreased to 60-69% during 2022-25 due to its capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 2 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was less than 1% of total revenue during 2023-25.
E Materials sourcing & efficiency 1
Sunonwealth’s sourcing of raw materials has been relatively stable (including IC, copper, plastic,
aluminium and steel), and we also note that Sunonwealth abides by RoHS regulations and
relevant international standards (ISO14001, IECQ QC080000, ISO9001) for its material sourcing
activities. With the ongoing ESG trend, its clients may move towards a more energy-efficient
production cycle, which will likely benefit Sunonwealth’s energy-efficient products.
E Product design & lifecycle management 2
Sunonwealth has improved its product designs to stronger but lighter form factors. Besides, we
have observed more green projects utilising Sunonwealth's capabilities. Thus, we believe
Sunonwealth has strong product design and lifecycle management capabilities to address the
ongoing changing business environment.
S Product quality & safety 2 Sunonwealth has its own Quality Strategy Centre to evaluate quality issues. With international standards in place, we believe Sunonwealth has taken relevant measures to address product quality and safety issues.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

## Document 233: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

(005930) 
삼성전자 
2026.09.29

반도체 손인준 
6154 / sij@eugenefn.com

긴 호흡으로 
 투자의견 STRONG BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 560,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 270,000원(9/28)

 삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익이 103.6조원(+16%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 메모리 가격 등 전반적인 반도체 업황에 대한 기존 가정은 대체로 
유지했으나, 최근 원화 강세에 따라 원/달러 환율 가정을 1,440원에서 
1,400원으로 낮추고 MX 사업부의 적자 확대를 반영해 실적 추정치를 하
향 조정했다. 또한 2Q26 재고가 3Q26 매출로 인식되는 과정에서 발생하
는 추가 성과급 충당금 부담도 반영했다.  
 DS 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 104.0조원(+17%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 서버 DRAM과 eSSD 중심의 가격 인상이 이어지며, DRAM과 
NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +17%, +18%QoQ 상승할 것으로 추정한
다. 1cnm 수율 개선과 HBM4의 빠른 램프업도 실적 개선에 기여할 전망이
다. Foundry/LSI는 HBM4 베이스다이 생산 확대, Groq 3 LPU 양산 및 선
단 공정 가격 인상에 힘입어 하반기 매출 성장이 예상된다. 다만 성과급 충
당금과 감가상각비 부담으로 영업적자 축소 폭은 제한적일 것으로 보인다. 
 DX 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 -1.8조원(적자지속qoq)을 기록할 것으로 추
정한다. 삼성전자는 메모리 가격 급등에도 제품 가격 인상을 제한하며 시장 
점유율 확대에 주력하고 있다. 이러한 가격 정책과 갤럭시 Z 폴드8 등 신제
품 흥행에 힘입어 판매량은 당초 예상치를 상회할 것으로 보인다. 다만 원
가 상승분의 전가가 제한되면서 영업적자 폭은 크게 확대될 전망이다.  
 삼성전자의 4Q26 영업이익은 114.9조원(+11%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. DRAM과 NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +11%, +12%QoQ 상승
하며 견조한 흐름을 이어갈 것으로 보인다. 환율 가정 조정에 따라 2026년
과 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 각각 365.2조원(+735%yoy), 595.8조원
(+63%YoY)으로 낮췄다. 4Q26~1Q27 범용 DRAM·NAND 및 2027년 
HBM 가격 협상 환경은 공급업체에 유리하게 전개되고 있어, 향후 가격 가
정의 추가 상향 가능성이 높다고 판단한다.  
 극심한 DRAM 공급 부족으로 인한 HBM 적층 단수 하향 조정(base die 수
요 증가), Memory Wall에 대응하기 위한 3D Stacked DRAM·zHBM 도입 
논의 등은 파운드리의 기술적 가치를 부각시키는 요인이다. 파운드리의 수
익성 회복 속도는 완만할 전망이지만, 메모리와 파운드리를 연계한 턴키 비
즈니스의 시너지는 AI 반도체 시장 내 경쟁력을 높이고, 메모리 시장 점유
율 확대에도 기여할 것으로 보인다. 적극적인 메모리 가격 인상 정책으로 
업계 최고 수준의 Blended ASP도 장기간 유지할 것으로 예상한다. 
 이제 메모리 업종 주가는 업황의 지속 가능성을 확인하고 꾸준한 주주환원
을 반영하며, 중장기적으로 점진적인 상승세를 이어갈 것으로 판단한다. 삼
성전자는 메모리·파운드리를 아우르는 턴키 역량과 가격 정책에 기반한 실
적 차별화를 통해 피어 그룹 내 경쟁력을 높여갈 것으로 보인다.

당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

## Document 278: AI忧虑冲击科技股，老虎环球、Lone Pine等选股基金10月惨遭重创

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI忧虑冲击科技股，老虎环球、Lone Pine等选股基金10月惨遭重创.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

受 AI 投资前景担忧及美国中期选举冲击，多家头部选股型对冲基⾦损失惨重： Lone Pine 亏
损约 9% 、年内转负；⽼虎环球跌 8.5% 、涨幅归零； Atreides 旗下对冲基⾦暴跌 16% 。
Coatue 逆势上涨 0.2% 成为少数亮点。多策略及量化基⾦跌幅相对温和。
摘要：
围绕⼈⼯智能投资的担忧引发科技股⼤规模抛售，多家头部选股型对冲基⾦在 10 ⽉遭受两位数
级别的损失，部分基⾦年内涨幅⼏近悉数回吐。
11 ⽉ 6 ⽇据彭博社援引知情⼈⼠透露， Steve Mandel 麾下Lone Pine Capital上⽉亏损约 9% ，此
前该基⾦已将年内⾼达 44% 的累计涨幅悉数抹去，⽬前 2026 年全年录得约 7% 的亏损，在同类基
⾦中表现居于末位。
⽼虎环球管理旗下对冲基⾦10 ⽉下跌 8.5% ，年内涨幅⼏乎归零；Whale Rock 资管公司旗舰基
⾦下挫约 8% ， 2026 年涨幅收窄⾄约 34% 。
与此同时，⺠主党在美国中期选举中取得压倒性胜利后，以科技股为主的纳斯达克综合指数周

四单⽇下跌 5.5% ，创 2025 年 4 ⽉以来最⼤跌幅，显⽰市场抛压仍在延续。
这场⻛波席卷多种对冲基⾦策略，芯⽚制造商股价受冲击尤为惨烈。中期选举实际上演变为市
场对新数据中⼼建设前景的⼀次公投， AI 融资忧虑由此转化为对科技板块的集中抛售。
Lone Pine 损失最重，年内收益全部蒸发
在此轮调整中， Lone Pine Capital 的处境最为艰难。
据报道，该基⾦ 10 ⽉亏损约 9% ，⽽在此之前，其年内涨幅已从⾼点的 44% 逐步被侵蚀殆尽，⽬
前 2026 年全年下跌约 7% ，在同类选股基⾦中跌幅居前。
相⽐之下，⽼虎环球旗下对冲基⾦10 ⽉下跌 8.5% 后，年内涨幅已基本归零。其规模较⼩的交叉
基⾦跌幅相对温和，上⽉下跌 3.3% ，年内仍保有 15.3% 的涨幅。
Whale Rock 旗舰公私混合基⾦下跌约 8% 后， 2026 年累计涨幅收窄⾄约 34% ；该公司旗下纯股
票多头基⾦跌幅更深， 10 ⽉重挫 12.3% ，年内涨幅收窄⾄ 30.6% 。

Dan Sundheim 麾下D1 Capital Partners 的股票投资组合上⽉下跌 4.4% ，年内回报率由此降⾄
18.8% 。
Atreides Management 旗下资产管理规模约 34 亿美元的旗舰公私混合基⾦10 ⽉下跌 9.5% ，降幅
在同类基⾦中位居前列。受益于对 SpaceX 及多家⾮上市公司的投资，该基⾦今年迄今仍录得约
43% 的涨幅。
Atreides旗下规模较⼩的对冲基⾦处境则更为窘迫 —— 上⽉重挫 16% ，年内涨幅⼤幅收窄⾄仅
剩 2.5% 。
Coatue 独善其⾝，多策略基⾦分化加剧
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

在选股基⾦普遍受挫的背景下， Philippe Laffont 的 Coatue Management 是为数不多的例外。其
对冲基⾦ 10 ⽉上涨 0.2% ，今年迄今累计上涨 13% ，在同类基⾦中表现相对突出。
从更⼴泛的对冲基⾦⾏业来看，各类策略之间的分化在 10 ⽉明显加剧。
多策略基⾦同样难以完全幸免： Balyasny 下跌 3.4% ， Millennium 下跌 2.3% ， ExodusPoint 下跌
2.4% ；⽽ Citadel Wellington 上涨 1.7% ， D.E. Shaw Composite 上涨 0.7% ，表现相对稳健。
量化基⾦与宏观基⾦整体跌幅较选股类基⾦更为温和， CFM Discus 、 Winton Diversified Macro
等⼩幅收涨，但 Marshall Wace TOPS 下跌 3.6% ， Kirkoswald 跌幅则⾼达 6.4% 。

### Page 1

PROPRIETARY RESEARCH FROM LSEG 
THIS WEEK IN EARNINGS

This Week in Earnings provides analysis and commentary on aggregate  
earnings estimate revisions, growth rates and valuations.

ANALYST: Tajinder Dhillon, CFA October 30, 2026 
tajinder.dhillon@lseg.com; 
THIS WEEK IN EARNINGS 
AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS 
 26Q3 Y/Y earnings are expected to be 31.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the Y/Y earnings estimate is 26.4%. 
 Of the 299 companies in the S&P 500 that have reported earnings to date for 26Q3, 81.6% have reported earnings 
above analyst estimates. This compares to a long-term average of 67.7% and prior four quarter average of 81.5%. 
 26Q3 Y/Y revenue is expected to be 13.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the growth estimate is 12.2%. 
 69.9% of companies have reported 26Q3 revenue above analyst expectations. This compares to a long-term 
average of 63% and an average over the past four quarters of 76.8%. 
 For 26Q3, there have been 40 negative EPS preannouncements issued by S&P 500 corporations compared to 66 
positive EPS preannouncements. By dividing 40 by 66 the N/P ratio is 0.6 for the S&P 500 Index.  
 The forward four-quarter (26Q4– 27Q3) P/E ratio for the S&P 500 is 19.0. 
 During the week of Nov. 2, 144 S&P 500 companies are expected to report quarterly earnings. 
SECTION A: EARNINGS OUTLOOK 
Q3 2026: EARNINGS REVISIONS 
EXHIBIT 1A.  S&P 500: Q3 2026 SHARE-WEIGHTED EARNINGS ($B)

### Page 2

PROPRIETARY RESEARCH FROM LSEG OCTOBER 30, 2026 
THIS WEEK IN EARNINGS 
This Week in Earnings provides analysis and commentary on aggregate earnings estimate revisions, growth rates and valuations.  2 
There has been an increase in the share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 since the start of the quarter (to $778.4B 
from $760.7B). Three of the eleven sectors have experienced downward revisions to estimates.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-20.5%) and materials (-7%) sectors have recorded the highest percentage 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (33%) sector has recorded the highest percentage increase in earnings. 
Overall, share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 2.3% since the start of the quarter.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-14.7B) and materials (-1.2B) sectors have recorded the highest dollar-level 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (15.7B) sector has recorded the highest dollar-level increase in earnings. 
Overall, expected share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 17.7B since the start of the quarter.

299 companies in the S&P 500 Index have reported earnings for Q3 2026. Of these companies, 81.6% reported 
earnings above analyst expectations and 15.7% reported earnings below analyst expectations. In a typical quarter 
(since 1994), 67% of companies beat estimates and 20% miss estimates. Over the past four quarters, 82% of 
companies beat the estimates and 15% missed estimates. 
In aggregate, companies are reporting earnings that are 4.5% above estimates, which compares to a long-term (since 
1994) average surprise factor of 4.4% and the average surprise factor over the prior four quarters of 7.7%.

299 companies in the S&P 500 Index have reported revenue for Q3 2026. Of these companies, 69.9% reported 
revenue above analyst expectations and 30.1% reported revenue below analyst expectations. In a typical quarter 
(since 2002), 63% of companies beat estimates and 37% miss estimates. Over the past four quarters, 77% of 
companies beat the estimates and 23% missed estimates. 
In aggregate, companies are reporting revenues that are 2.4% above estimates, which compares to a long-term (since 
2002) average surprise factor of 1.3% and the average surprise factor over the prior four quarters of 2.5%.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the S&P 500 for 26Q3 is 31.6%. If the energy sector is excluded, the growth 
rate declines to 26.4%. The S&P 500 expects to see share-weighted earnings of $778.4B in 26Q3, compared to share-
weighted earnings of $591.4B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 constituents) in 25Q3. 
11 of the 11 sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to 25Q3. The Energy and 
Information Technology sectors have the highest earnings growth rates for the quarter, while the Consumer 
Discretionary sector has the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q3. 
The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (146.2%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $63B in 26Q3, 
compared to earnings of $25.6B in 25Q3. 4 of the 5 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher earnings 
than a year ago. The Oil & Gas Refining & Marketing (566.5%) and Integrated Oil & Gas (143.8%) sub-industries have 
the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate declines to 39%. 
The Information Technology sector has the second highest earnings growth rate (68.9%) of any sector. It is expected 
to earn $260.2B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $154B in 25Q3. 12 of the 12 sub-industries in the sector are 
anticipated to see higher earnings than a year ago. The Semiconductors (137%) and Semiconductor Materials & 
Equipment (71.5%) sub-industries have the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, 
the growth rate declines to 28.2%. 
The Consumer Discretionary sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (3.9%) of any sector. It is expected to earn 
$56.3B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $54.2B in 25Q3. 7 of the 18 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to 
see lower earnings than a year ago. The Casinos & Gaming (-44.1%) and Automotive Parts & Equipment (-38.8%) 
sub-industries have the lowest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate 
improves to 4.5%.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the S&P 500 for 26Q4 is 29.7%. If the energy sector is excluded, the growth 
rate declines to 25%. The S&P 500 expects to see share-weighted earnings of $810.7B in 26Q4, compared to share-
weighted earnings of $625.1B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 constituents) in 25Q4. 
11 of the 11 sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to 25Q4. The Energy and 
Information Technology sectors have the highest earnings growth rates for the quarter, while the Industrials sector has 
the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q4. 
The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (145.6%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $59.5B in 26Q4, 
compared to earnings of $24.2B in 25Q4. 3 of the 5 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher earnings 
than a year ago. The Oil & Gas Refining & Marketing (366.3%) and Integrated Oil & Gas (175.7%) sub-industries have 
the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate declines to 52.1%. 
The Information Technology sector has the second highest earnings growth rate (56.3%) of any sector. It is expected 
to earn $292.1B in 26Q4, compared to earnings of $186.8B in 25Q4. 12 of the 12 sub-industries in the sector are 
anticipated to see higher earnings than a year ago. The Semiconductors (119.5%) and Semiconductor Materials & 
Equipment (72.3%) sub-industries have the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, 
the growth rate declines to 19.1%. 
The Industrials sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (3.9%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $52B in 26Q4, 
compared to earnings of $50.1B in 25Q4. 2 of the 20 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see lower earnings 
than a year ago. The Aerospace & Defense (-39.6%) and Passenger Airlines (-29.8%) sub-industries have the lowest 
earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate improves to 30.5%.

The estimated revenue growth rate for the S&P 500 for 26Q3 is 13.6%. If the energy sector is excluded, the growth rate declines  to 12.2%. The S&P 
500 expects to see revenue of $4898.8B in 26Q3, compared to revenue of $4310.4B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 
constituents) in 25Q3.

11 of the 11 sectors anticipate revenue growth for the quarter. The Information Technology sector has the highest revenue growth rate for the 
quarter, while the Financials sector has the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q3.

The Information Technology sector has the highest revenue growth rate (40.7%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $772.3B in 26Q3, compared to 
revenue of $548.8B in 25Q3. 12 of the 12 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher revenue than a year ago. The Semiconductors 
(88%) and Semiconductor Materials & Equipment (49.4%) sub-industries have the highest revenue growth in the sector. If these su b-industries are 
removed, the growth rate declines to 23.7%.

The Financials sector has the lowest revenue growth rate (5.1%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $576.7B in 26Q3, compared  to revenue of 
$548.4B in 25Q3. 3 of the 13 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see lower revenue than a year ago. The Diversified  Financial Services (-
40.9%) and Reinsurance (-18.1%) sub-industries have the lowest revenue growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are remove d, the growth rate 
improves to 6%.

STARMINE EARNINGS SURPRISE FORECAST
Looking forward at quarterly performance, we use StarMine’s SmartEstimate® from LSEG to determine which
companies in the S&P 500 are better poised to beat and miss earnings estimates. The SmartEstimate® is a weighted

average of analyst estimates, with more weight given to more recent estimates and more accurate analysts. Our
studies have shown that when the SmartEstimate® differs significantly from the consensus (I/B/E/S mean estimate),
the Predicted Surprise accurately predicts the direction of earnings surprises or further revisions 70% of the time.
When a significant Predicted Surprise for revenue is also present for the period, the accuracy improves to 78%.

StarMine ARM from LSEG is an analyst revisions stock ranking model, designed to predict future changes in analyst
sentiment. ARM incorporates more accurate earnings estimates through the SmartEstimate prediction service. ARM
region rankings scores companies by region on a scale of 1 to 100 where 100 represents the most bullish sentiment.
Over the next two weeks, 147 S&P 500 companies are expected to report earnings. Of these companies, 17 positive

surprises and 11 negative surprises are expected from S&P 500 companies reporting quarterly results.

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Charts of the week: Watching earnings and a potential ‘Blue wave’

US Big Tech earnings, mid-term elections and major central bank policy meetings are the near-term market drivers 
Consensus S&P500 index Q3 earnings estimates by sector   Chance of Democrats winning control of US House and Senate*

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *Polymarket probability

Editorial 
Policy, politics and earnings  
Strategy summary:  We retain our near -term defensive and 
diversified stance across assets, sectors and regions amid 
near-term uncertainty around monetary policy, politics and 
earnings. The next two weeks will be busy for major central 
banks, US political watchers and technology leaders powering 
the AI supercycle. We expect the Fed, ECB and BoJ to hold 
rates next week, but markets will focus on whether central 
banks are turning more hawkish due to the latest surge in fuel 
prices. The US mid -term elections are expected to give 
Democrats control of the House and Senate, with implications 
for taxes and the AI-led investment boom.  
Meanwhile, the consensus is expecting the US tech sector to 
report over 60% y/y earnings growth for Q3, the biggest jump 
in over two decades outside the post-COVID rebound.  Each of 
these three drivers have the potential to surprise markets.  
Big Three to hold monetary policy: We expect the Fed, ECB 
and BoJ to hold policy rates next week, after September’s 
hikes, as they assess whether higher bond yields have 
tightened financial conditions enough to offset fuel-price-driven 
inflation. We expect the Big Three to hike next in December as 
fuel prices are likely to stay elevated into early next year due to 
refining capacity constraints. Nevertheless, the Fed and ECB 
are likely to deliver less than around 60bps of rate hikes priced 
in by money markets over the next 12 months on the back of 
an eventual easing of oil and fuel prices leading to disinflation 
by H2 2027. This outlook means 3 -7-year Developed Market 
investment grade bonds currently offer attractive risk-reward.  
Opportunity in Australian bonds. Other major central banks, 
such as the Bank of England, Bank of Canada and Reserve 
Bank of New Zealand are likely to hike rates by 25bps in the 
next two weeks given their focus on taming near-term inflation. 
However, we believe the Reserve Bank of Australia (RBA) is 
close to the end of its hiking cycle, even if it hikes one last time 
in the coming months (money markets give a 60% chance of a 
hike on 3 November). Australia’s domestic labour and housing 
markets and business confidence are softening after the RBA 
hiked its policy rate four times this year to a 15 -year high. 
Meanwhile, Australia’s cash rate is 60bps higher than the US 
policy rate, supporting the AUD. We continue to see attractive 
income and FX gain opportunities from AUD corporate bonds.  
A ‘Blue Wave’ in the US?  Prediction markets strongly favour 
Democrats taking the House of Representatives and possibly 
the Senate in the 3 November US mid-term elections. While an 
opposition sweep in the mid-terms has historically delivered the 
best 12-month returns for equities  among all sc enarios, partly 
due to the resulting policy gridlock, a decisive Democratic “blue 
wave” could increase longer -term risks of higher taxes and 
uncertainty over policy towards AI investment and data centres. 
With the Senate race still close and prediction markets fa llible, 
we remain cautious on risk assets in the near term.  
Closing EU bank contingent convertible bond s (CoCos1) 
and US 10 -year go vernment bond ideas:  We lock in 2% 
gains in EU CoCos and 1.7% gain in US 10 -year government 
bond ideas amid French and US political risks (page 5-6). We 
continue to hedge any inflation risks through inflation-protected 
bonds and geopolitical risk through an Overweight in gold.  
Watching Big Tech earnings:   Upcoming Big Tech earnings 
raises uncertainty as market leadership remains concentrated 
and share -price reactions more mixed. With valuations less 
likely to drive returns, earnings delivery and sustainability of AI 
investment, funding and monet isation and broadening out of 
earnings beyond tech will be critical, raising the risk of volatility 
and sector divergence.  We remain invested but defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification across sectors and regions.  
Staying defensive and diversified. We recently upgraded the 
defensive US Healthcare sector to Overweight , while retaining 
our Overweights to US tech and materials as well as financials 
in the US, Euro area and Japan. We also downgraded the more 
volatile Communications Services sector to Neutral  and took 
profits on the semiconductor segment earlier this month before 
the latest correction. Our defensive and diversified stance 
should preserve participation in further equity upside, while 
reducing exposure to earnings disappointments and supporting 
resilience through the reporting season.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions 
  What are the key takeaways from the technology sector 
earnings reported so far this week? 
Our view: Recent  technology sector earnings results point to 
strong compute demand and continued capex expansion, 
supporting our positive long -term view on the AI investment 
theme. However, rising funding and execution risks warrant 
near-term caution. We prefer to avoid increasing exposure to 
higher-risk areas within the technology sector for now. 
Rationale: Earnings results reported this week suggest major tech 
companies remain committed to expanding their AI infrastructure. 
While this bodes well for AI infrastructure providers, ambitious 
expansion plans imply that an increasing share of AI capex is likely 
to depend on debt financing. We expect debt-financed AI capex to 
rise from c.25% of AI capex in 2026 to c.40% in 2027.  
Greater reliance on borrowing increases sensitivity to interest rates, 
while deployment delays could postpone revenue recognition amid 
ongoing financing costs. However, evidence of improving AI 
monetisation and adoption should strengthen operating cash 
flows and support internal capex funding. Against this backdrop, 
we will assess Big Tech earnings next week for greater clarity on 
capex guidance and funding and AI monetisation trends.  
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  Are you still positive on Euro  area bank equities amid 
heightened political and fiscal uncertainty in the region? 
Our view: We remain positive on Euro  area bank equities, 
supported by resilient earnings and shareholder-friendly capital 
return policies. While Fr ance’s fiscal uncertainty and a softer 
growth outlook warrant caution, the recent pullback in Euro  
area bank equities  offers selective opportunities. We favour 
well-capitalised banks with strong deposit franchises and 
diversified earnings. 
Rationale: We expect Euro  area banks’ Q3 2026 net interest 
income to grow 1.5 -3.0% q/q , led by Spanish banks, driven by 
higher rates and continued asset repricing. Resilient fee income from 
wealth management and investment banking activity, as well as 
dividends and buybacks, should provide further support. Price-to-
book (P/B) valuations have eased , despite expectations for 
healthy return on equity (ROE), thus improving risk-reward. 
Fiscal uncertainty in France has widened sovereign spreads, 
pressuring banks’ bond holdings and funding costs . Although 
French banks represent 13% of the Euro  area Bank Index, volatility 
could spread to regional financial markets. However, stress in the 
broader banking system appears limited , supported by strong 
capital buffers and ECB tools to contain disorderly market moves and 
sovereign market contagion. We will continue to monitor credit 
trends, sovereign spreads and banks’ 2027 earnings guidance. 
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What has driven the recent weakness in  China equities, 
and what is your outlook from here? 
Our view: We maintain China equities as  a Core holding , as 
valuations remain inexpensive. Rapid technological innovation 
and investment in  AI localisation provide tailwinds, but the 
potential for a re-rating appears delayed. Investors should seek 
to diversify their portfolios and reduce concentration risk. 
Rationale: China equities have weak ened, driven by mounting 
scepticism over AI capex returns, structural margin 
compression and concerns about the country’s significant 
financing requirements. Another notable feature in China has been 
significant share issuance, which has diluted earnings per share and 
weighed on the MSCI China Index’s performance. These pressures 
have been compounded by geopolitical headwinds, restrictions on 
advanced chips, an enduring property sect or contraction and weak 
domestic demand. September’s stimulus measures also 
disappointed markets in terms of their scale and pace of delivery. 
Notwithstanding these headwinds, valuations remain inexpensive, 
supported by rapid domestic innovation and structural tailwinds 
in AI localisation , leading us to retain China equities as a Core 
holding. Elevated volatility reinforces the need to be defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification rather than relying on technology 
sector concentration. We also remain Overweight China’s 
healthcare, financial and materials sectors. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Are you still bullish on US 10-year government bonds?   
Our view: We are closing our opportunistic bullish idea on US 
10-year government bonds at a gain of 1.7%. The risk -reward 
has become less compelling after the yield broke below 5%, the 
lower bound of our three-month forecast range of 5.00-5.25%. 
Rationale: The US 10 -year yield fell  over 17bps to 4.97% on 22 
October – its first move below 5% since 23 September and biggest 
one-day drop since March 2023. A sell-off in AI-related equities and 
renewed Middle East tensions drove investors towards safe havens. 
Earlier this month, the yield hit its highest level in over two decades. 
The forces behind the rally are fragile, though.  Oil prices remain 
elevated amid escalating Middle East tensions, and September US 
consumer inflation rose to 3.7% y/y . Fed officials  have signalled 
there is no urgency to raise rates at the 27-28 October meeting, 
but persistent price pressures keep further tightening on the table. 
We prefer to lock in gains and close our opportunistic bullish idea on 
10-year government bonds at a gain of 1.7%. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO, Fixed Income & FX

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is your outlook for French bank bonds , especially 
ahead of France’s upcoming sovereign credit rating reviews?   
Our view: The recent sell-off in French bank bonds is a repricing 
of tail risks, not a credit story. We expect further price volatility 
until the April 2027 French presidential election, particularly at 
the junior end of the capital structure, so we are closing our 
opportunistic EU bank contingent convertibles (CoCos1) idea at 
a gain of 2.0%. 
Rationale: We believe the recent sell -off in French bank bonds 
reflects domestic political and fiscal risks rather than weaker 
credit fundamentals . The sustained widening of the OAT -Bund 
spread has raised concerns about the profit and loss (P&L) impact 
on banks' sovereign debt holdings. Banks provide limited 
disclosure on these exposures. However, banks’ investment 
portfolios are generally diversified, and holdings booked at amortised 
cost should soften the P&L impact. Near -term catalysts include the 
upcoming sovereign credit rating rev iews. A sovereign downgrade 
could trigger bank rating downgrades, with domestically focused 
lenders potentially more exposed than regionally diversified peers. A 
sovereign credit event is not our base case, but we expect headline 
risks to persist until the  French presidential election . Against this 
backdrop, we are closing our opportunistic EU bank CoCos idea. 
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Will the November US mid-term elections and European 
bond volatility support a sustained Swiss franc (CHF) rebound?  
Our view: We expect a near -term CHF rebound, supported 
mainly by safe -haven demand amid European bond volatility. 
We believe the US mid-term elections will be a secondary driver. 
Rationale: European sovereign bond volatility provides the stronger 
catalyst for CHF strength. The Fr ance-Germany government bond 
yield spread remains elevated near 160bps, reflecting heightened 
French fiscal concerns and reinforcing the CHF’s safe-haven appeal. 
The US mid-terms could provide additional support for the CHF. 
A Democratic victory in either chamber is expected to  constrain 
further fiscal expansion, potentially weighing on the USD. However, 
this would not necessarily lead to faster fiscal consolidation. 
The SNB’s 0% policy rate leaves  the CHF at a sizeable interest -
rate disadvantage vs. the USD, constraining sustained CHF gains. 
With EUR/CHF below 0.90 , excessive CHF strength could also 
increase the risk of SNB intervention . A decisive USD/CHF break 
below 0.8130 would strengthen the technical case for a more 
sustained CHF rebound.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

A widening France-Germany 10-year government 
bond yield spread and a stable investment-grade 
(IG) bond option-adjusted spread (OAS) suggest a 
repricing of political and fiscal risk rather than 
credit risk

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Would worsening budget deficits and political uncertainty 
across parts of Europe affect your view on EUR/USD and Euro 
area government bond yields? 
Our view: Fiscal and political concerns constrain near -term 
EUR/USD gains, with France being the primary vulnerability. We 
expect EUR/USD to recover towards the 1.17-1.18 range over 
the next  12 months, supported by continued Euro area 
economic growth and an expected fading of USD support. We 
expect wider differentials in Euro area government bond yields, 
with political risk premium elevated in Fr ench government 
bonds (OATs) and German government bonds (Bunds) 
supported by safe-haven demand. 
Rationale: France's high  public debt, large budget deficit and 
political obstacles to fiscal adjustment s are  weighing on market 
confidence, with the country’s  10-year government bond yield 
rising above 5%. The risk of stress spreading to Italy and Spain 
adds to regional concerns, although current evidence does not 
establish a broad European fiscal crisis. German government bonds 
should benefit from safe-haven demand, while fiscal concerns keep 
risk premi a on vulnerable countries' bonds elevated. EUR/USD 
remains more sensitive to US-Germany interest-rate differentials 
and broader market confidence than to French bond yields alone. 
France’s fiscal risks, e levated energy costs and high US interest 
rates weigh on the EUR in the near term. Over the next 12 months, 
Germany’s multi -year public investment programme, resilient 
exports and sound banking-sector fundamentals are expected to 
support Euro area growth. We expect the Fed's rate-hiking cycle to 
peak by mid -2027, reducing USD support and driving  the EUR  
towards 1.17-1.18 . 
The principal tail risk  is fiscal stress widening government bond 
yield spreads and spilling over into bank funding  conditions and 
lending activity, weakening growth and delaying the EUR's recovery. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

France’s fiscal risks constrain near-term EUR/USD 
gains, but a modest recovery remains likely
